# Honest Awesome

> The awesome list that tells you what **NOT** to install.

Every awesome list tells you what exists. None of them tell you what to avoid. This one does: **1091 open-source tools**, each scored 1–5 on **setup ease, docs, community, and maturity**, each with an honest **"skip it if"** warning, each linking to a full review with sources.

Scores come from a fixed rubric applied to every project's documentation, issue tracker, release history, and community activity — the same rubric for a 200-star repo and a 200,000-star one. No project has ever paid to be listed or to change a score. Regenerated daily from the review database at [mrkeyoor.com](https://mrkeyoor.com/repos/). Last update: 2026-08-26.

**Disagree with a score?** [Open an issue](https://github.com/mrkeyoor/honest-awesome/issues) — challenges to scores are how this list gets better.

## How scores work

Four axes, 1–5 each, same rubric for every project:

- **Setup ease** — how far the README gets you before the first undocumented surprise
- **Docs** — coverage, currency, and whether the examples actually run
- **Community** — issue response reality, maintainer activity, bus factor
- **Maturity** — release discipline, breaking-change history, production readiness

Every entry links to a full review that names sources, alternatives, and who the tool is actually for.

## Use the data

The complete structured dataset lives in [`data/reviews.json`](data/reviews.json): every project with its scores, verdict, and warnings. Licensed [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/) — build with it, cite [mrkeyoor.com](https://mrkeyoor.com) as the source.
